F.3.1
Results in Section
4.1

Proof of Proposition
4.1
.

The following three lemmas follow from Lemmas
F.6
–
F.8

with
\widehat{\mathbf{S}}=\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{x}(0)
and
\mathbf{S}=\bm{\Gamma}_{\chi}(0)
,
as conditions (
F.6
)–(
F.9
) are met by
Assumption
4.1

(ii)
,
Proposition
2.1
and Theorem
3.1
.

Lemma F.19
.

There exists an orthonormal, diagonal matrix

\bm{\mathcal{O}}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times r}
such that

\displaystyle\frac{\inf_{\omega}\mu_{\chi,q}^{2}(\omega)\cdot\mu_{\chi,r}}{p^{3}}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{\chi}-\mathbf{E}_{\chi}\bm{\mathcal{O}}\right\|=O_{P}\left(\psi_{n}\vee\frac{1}{m}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right).

Lemma F.20
.

\displaystyle\frac{\inf_{\omega}\mu_{\chi,q}^{2}(\omega)\cdot\mu^{2}_{\chi,r}}{p^{4}}\left\|\left(\frac{\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{M}}}_{\chi}}{p}\right)^{-1}-\left(\frac{\bm{\mathcal{M}}_{\chi}}{p}\right)^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\psi_{n}\vee\frac{1}{m}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right).

We frequently use that by Lemma
F.2
and
(
F.3
)–(
F.4
), together with Chebyshev’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{X}_{t}\|=O_{P}(\sqrt{p}),\quad\|\bm{\chi}_{t}\|=O_{P}(\sqrt{p})\quad\text{and}\quad\|\bm{\xi}_{t}\|=O_{P}(\sqrt{p})

(F.23)

for any given
t
.
Note that

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{\bm{\chi}}^{\text{{res}}}_{n+a|n}-\bm{\chi}_{n+a|n}\right|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq i\leq p}\left|\bm{\varphi}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\Gamma}_{\chi}(-a)\mathbf{E}_{\chi}\bm{\mathcal{M}}_{\chi}^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{\chi}^{\top}\bm{\xi}_{n}\right|

\displaystyle+\max_{i}\left|\bm{\varphi}_{i}^{\top}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{\chi}(-a)-\bm{\Gamma}_{\chi}(-a)\right)\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{\chi}\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{M}}}_{\chi}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{\chi}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{n}\right|

\displaystyle+\max_{i}\left|\bm{\varphi}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\Gamma}_{\chi}(-a)\left(\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{\chi}-\mathbf{E}_{\chi}\bm{\mathcal{O}}\right)\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{M}}}_{\chi}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{\chi}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{n}\right|

\displaystyle+\max_{i}\left|\bm{\varphi}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\Gamma}_{\chi}(-a)\mathbf{E}_{\chi}\bm{\mathcal{O}}\left(\widehat{\bm{\mathcal{M}}}_{\chi}^{-1}-\bm{\mathcal{M}}^{-1}_{\chi}\right)\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{\chi}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{n}\right|

\displaystyle+\max_{i}\left|\bm{\varphi}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\Gamma}_{\chi}(-a)\mathbf{E}_{\chi}\bm{\mathcal{O}}\bm{\mathcal{M}}^{-1}_{\chi}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{\chi}-\mathbf{E}_{\chi}\bm{\mathcal{O}}\right)^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{n}\right|=I+II+III+IV+V.

By (
F.3
), Assumption
4.1

(ii)

and combining the observation

\|\mathsf{Cov}(\mathbf{E}_{\chi}^{\top}\bm{\xi}_{t})\|\leq 2\pi B_{\xi}
from Proposition
2.1

with Chebyshev’s inequality,
we have
I=O_{P}(\sqrt{p}/\mu_{\chi,r})
.
Using the arguments in the proof of Proposition
F.16
,
the result therein is extended to showing the consistency of
\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{\chi}(a)
at a given lag
a
.
Then combined with
Lemmas
F.20
and (
F.23
), it leads to

\displaystyle II=O_{P}\left(\frac{p^{3}}{\inf_{\omega}\mu_{\chi,q}^{2}(\omega)\cdot\mu_{\chi,r}}\cdot\left(\vartheta_{n,p}\vee\frac{1}{m}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right)\right).

Next, from (
F.3
), (
F.23
)
and Lemmas
F.19
–
F.20
,

\displaystyle III=O_{P}\left(\frac{p^{4}}{\inf_{\omega}\mu_{\chi,q}^{2}(\omega)\cdot\mu^{2}_{\chi,r}}\cdot\left(\psi_{n}\vee\frac{1}{m}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right)\right),

and
V
is handled analogously. Finally,
from (
F.3
), (
F.23
) and Lemma
F.20
,

\displaystyle IV=O_{P}\left(\frac{p^{4}}{\inf_{\omega}\mu_{\chi,q}^{2}(\omega)\cdot\mu^{2}_{\chi,r}}\cdot\left(\psi_{n}\vee\frac{1}{m}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right)\right).

Putting together the bounds on
I
–
V
, the conclusion follows.
∎

We prove the following proposition which includes Proposition
4.2
as a special case.

Proposition F.21
.

Suppose that Assumptions
2.2
and
F.1
hold.
Then for any
a\geq 1
,

\displaystyle|\bm{\chi}_{n+a|n}-\bm{\chi}_{n+a}|_{\infty}=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}O_{P}\left(q^{1/\nu}\mu_{\nu}^{1/\nu}\log^{1/2}(p)\right)&\text{under Assumption~\ref{assum:innov}~\ref{cond:dist}~\ref{cond:moment},}\\
O_{P}\left(\log^{1/2}(p)\right)&\text{under Assumption~\ref{assum:innov}~\ref{cond:dist}~\ref{cond:gauss}.}\end{array}\right.

Proof.

From (
14
) and Assumption
F.1

(ii)
,

\bm{\chi}_{n+a|n}-\bm{\chi}_{n+a}=\sum_{\ell=0}^{a-1}\mathbf{B}_{\ell}\mathbf{u}_{n+a-\ell}
for all
a\geq 1
.
By Lemma D.3 of
Zhang and Wu, 2021
,
Assumptions
2.2
and
F.1
,
there exist constants
C_{\nu},C_{\nu,\Xi,\varsigma}>0

that depend only on their subscripts such that

\displaystyle\left\|\left|\sum_{\ell=0}^{a-1}\mathbf{B}_{\ell}\mathbf{u}_{n+a-\ell}\right|_{\infty}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}=\left\|\left|\sum_{\ell=0}^{a-1}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\mathbf{B}_{\ell,\cdot j}u_{j,n+a-\ell}\right|_{\infty}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}\leq C_{\nu}\log(p)\sum_{\ell=0}^{a-1}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\left\|\left|\mathbf{B}_{\ell,\cdot j}u_{j,n+a-\ell}\right|_{\infty}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\nu}\log(p)\sum_{\ell=0}^{a-1}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\left|\mathbf{B}_{\ell,\cdot j}\right|_{\infty}^{2}\left\|\left|\mathbf{u}_{t+a-\ell}\right|_{\infty}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}\leq C_{\nu}\log(p)\sum_{\ell=0}^{a-1}\Xi^{2}(1+\ell)^{-2\varsigma}q^{2/\nu}\mu_{\nu}^{2/\nu}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\nu,\Xi,\varsigma}\log(p)q^{2/\nu}\mu_{\nu}^{2/\nu}

under Assumption
F.1

(iii)

(iii)(a)
,
such that by Chebyshev’s inequality, we have

\left|\sum_{\ell=0}^{a-1}\mathbf{B}_{\ell}\mathbf{u}_{n+a-\ell}\right|_{\infty}=O_{P}(\log^{1/2}(p)q^{1/\nu}\mu_{\nu}^{1/\nu})
.
When Assumption
F.1

(iii)

(iii)(b)
holds,
notice that

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq i\leq p}\mathsf{Var}\left(\sum_{\ell=0}^{a-1}\mathbf{B}_{\ell,i\cdot}\mathbf{u}_{t+a-\ell}\right)=\max_{i}\sum_{\ell=0}^{a-1}\left|\mathbf{B}_{\ell,i\cdot}\right|_{2}^{2}\leq\sum_{\ell=0}^{a-1}\Xi^{2}(1+\ell)^{-\varsigma}<\infty

such that by Gaussian maximal inequality, we have

\left|\sum_{\ell=0}^{a-1}\mathbf{B}_{\ell}\mathbf{u}_{n+a-\ell}\right|_{\infty}=O_{P}(\log^{1/2}(p))
.
∎

F.3.2
Proof of Proposition
4.3

Let
\widetilde{\zeta}^{(1)}_{p}=\log^{1/2}(p)p^{1/\nu}\mu_{\nu}^{1/\nu}
and
\widetilde{\zeta}^{(2)}_{p}=p^{1/\nu}\mu_{\nu}^{1/\nu}
under Assumption
F.1

(iii)

(iii)(a)
,
\widetilde{\zeta}^{(1)}_{p}=\widetilde{\zeta}^{(2)}_{p}=\log^{1/2}(p)
under Assumption
F.1

(iii)

(iii)(b)
.
We first show that for any given
t
,

\displaystyle|\bm{\xi}_{t}|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\widetilde{\zeta}^{(1)}_{p}\right)\quad\text{and}\quad|\bm{\Gamma}^{1/2}\bm{\varepsilon}_{t}|_{\infty}=O_{P}(\widetilde{\zeta}^{(2)}_{p}),

with which we establish

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{\bm{\xi}}_{n+1|n}-\bm{\xi}_{n+1}\right|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(s_{\text{in}}\zeta_{n,p}\widetilde{\zeta}^{(1)}_{p}+\|\bm{\beta}\|_{1}\bar{\zeta}_{n,p}+p^{1/\nu}\widetilde{\zeta}^{(2)}_{p}\right).

(F.24)

Under Assumption
F.1

(iii)

(iii)(a)
,
by (
F.2
) and Assumption
2.3
,
we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left|\mathbf{D}_{\ell}\bm{\Gamma}^{1/2}\bm{\varepsilon}_{t-\ell}\right|_{\infty}\right\|_{\nu}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\nu}M_{\varepsilon}^{1/2}\Xi(1+\ell)^{-\varsigma}p^{1/\nu}\mu_{\nu}^{1/\nu}

such that applying Lemma D.3 of
Zhang and Wu, 2021
, we have

\displaystyle\||\bm{\xi}_{t}|_{\infty}\|_{\nu}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\left|\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\mathbf{D}_{\ell}\bm{\Gamma}^{1/2}\bm{\varepsilon}_{t-\ell}\right|_{\infty}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}\leq C_{\nu}^{\prime}\log(p)\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\left\|\left|\mathbf{D}_{\ell}\bm{\Gamma}^{1/2}\bm{\varepsilon}_{t-\ell}\right|_{\infty}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\nu}^{2}C_{\nu}^{\prime}M_{\varepsilon}\log(p)p^{2/\nu}\mu_{\nu}^{2/\nu}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\Xi^{2}(1+\ell)^{-2\varsigma}\leq C_{\nu,\Xi,\varsigma}M_{\varepsilon}\log(p)p^{2/\nu}\mu_{\nu}^{2/\nu}

with
C_{\nu},C^{\prime}_{\nu},C_{\nu,\Xi,\varsigma}
denoting some positive constants
that depend only on their subscripts.
Therefore, by Chebyshev’s inequality,

\widetilde{\zeta}^{(1)}_{p}=\log^{1/2}(p)p^{1/\nu}\mu_{\nu}^{1/\nu}
.
Similarly, by Assumption
2.3
and Lemma D.3 of
Zhang and Wu, 2021
,

\displaystyle\left\|(\bm{\Gamma}^{1/2})_{i\cdot}\bm{\varepsilon}_{t}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}=\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{p}(\bm{\Gamma}^{1/2})_{ik}\varepsilon_{kt}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}\leq C_{\nu}|(\bm{\Gamma}^{1/2})_{i\cdot}|_{2}^{2}\mu_{\nu}^{2/\nu}\leq C_{\nu}M_{\varepsilon}\mu_{\nu}^{2/\nu}

for all
1\leq i\leq p
, such that

\||\bm{\Gamma}^{1/2}\bm{\varepsilon}_{t}|_{\infty}\|_{\nu}\leq(C_{\nu}M_{\varepsilon})^{1/2}p^{1/\nu}\mu_{\nu}^{1\nu}

and with Chebyshev’s inequality,
we have
\widetilde{\zeta}^{(2)}_{p}=p^{1/\nu}\mu_{\nu}^{1/\nu}
.

Under Assumption
F.1

(iii)

(iii)(b)
,
by Gaussian maximal inequality and that
\mu_{\xi,1}\leq 2\pi B_{\xi}

(which indicates that
\max_{1\leq i\leq p}\mathsf{Var}(\xi_{it})\leq 2\pi B_{\xi}
),
we have
\widetilde{\zeta}^{(1)}_{p}=\log^{1/2}(p)
and similarly,

\widetilde{\zeta}^{(2)}_{p}=\log^{1/2}(p)
.

Next, by construction, we have

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{\bm{\xi}}_{n+1|n}-\bm{\xi}_{n+1|n}\right|_{\infty}\leq\left|\sum_{\ell=1}^{d}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}_{\ell}-\mathbf{A}_{\ell}\right)\widehat{\bm{\xi}}_{n+1-\ell}\right|_{\infty}+\left|\sum_{\ell=1}^{d}\mathbf{A}_{\ell}\left(\widehat{\bm{\xi}}_{n+1-\ell}-\bm{\xi}_{n+1-\ell}\right)\right|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\ell=1}^{d}\left\|\left(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}_{\ell}-\mathbf{A}_{\ell}\right)^{\top}\right\|_{1}\left(\left|\widehat{\bm{\xi}}_{n+1-\ell}-\bm{\xi}_{n+1-\ell}\right|_{\infty}+\left|\bm{\xi}_{n+1-\ell}\right|_{\infty}\right)+\sum_{\ell=1}^{d}\left\|\mathbf{A}_{\ell}^{\top}\right\|_{1}\left|\widehat{\bm{\xi}}_{n+1-\ell}-\bm{\xi}_{n+1-\ell}\right|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-\bm{\beta}\right\|_{1}\;\max_{1\leq\ell\leq d}\left(\left|\widehat{\bm{\xi}}_{n+1-\ell}-\bm{\xi}_{n+1-\ell}\right|_{\infty}+\left|\bm{\xi}_{n+1-\ell}\right|_{\infty}\right)+\left\|\bm{\beta}\right\|_{1}\;\max_{1\leq\ell\leq d}\left|\widehat{\bm{\xi}}_{n+1-\ell}-\bm{\xi}_{n+1-\ell}\right|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(s_{\text{in}}\zeta_{n,p}\left(\bar{\zeta}_{n,p}+\widetilde{\zeta}^{(1)}_{p}\right)+\|\bm{\beta}\|_{1}\bar{\zeta}_{n,p}\right),

where it is used that

\max_{1\leq\ell\leq d}|\bm{\xi}_{t+1-\ell}|_{\infty}=O_{P}(d\widetilde{\zeta}^{(1)}_{p})

for given
t
.
This, combined with the observation that
\bm{\Gamma}^{1/2}\bm{\varepsilon}_{n+1}=\bm{\xi}_{n+1|n}-\bm{\xi}_{n+1}
, proves (
F.24
).

F.3.3
Results in Section
B

Proof of Proposition
B.1
.

Let
\bm{\Sigma}_{w}(\omega)
denote the spectral density matrix of
\mathbf{W}_{t}
. Then,

\bm{\Sigma}_{w}(\omega)=\mathcal{A}_{\chi}(e^{-\iota\omega})\bm{\Sigma}_{\xi}(\omega)\mathcal{A}_{\chi}^{\top}(e^{\iota\omega})

and for any
\mathbf{a}\in\mathbb{C}^{p}
with
|\mathbf{a}|_{2}^{2}=1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{a}^{*}\bm{\Sigma}_{w}(\omega)\mathbf{a}\leq\mu_{\xi,1}(\omega)\;\mathbf{a}^{*}\mathcal{A}_{\chi}(e^{-\iota\omega})\mathcal{A}_{\chi}^{\top}(e^{\iota\omega})\mathbf{a}\leq B_{\xi}\|\mathcal{A}_{\chi}(e^{-\iota\omega})\mathcal{A}_{\chi}^{\top}(e^{\iota\omega})\|,

thanks to Proposition
2.1
.
Further, by Assumption
B.2

(ii)

and Lemma
F.2
, there exists a constant
B_{\chi}>0
such that

\displaystyle\sup_{\omega\in[-\pi,\pi]}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\chi}(e^{-\iota\omega})\mathcal{A}_{\chi}^{\top}(e^{\iota\omega})\right\|\leq\max_{1\leq h\leq N}\sup_{\omega\in[-\pi,\pi]}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{\chi}^{(h)}(e^{-\iota\omega})(\mathcal{A}_{\chi}^{(h)}(e^{\iota\omega}))^{\top}\right\|\leq B_{\chi}.

Consequently,

\displaystyle\mu_{w,1}=\sup_{\mathbf{a}\in\mathbb{R}^{p}}\mathbf{a}^{\top}\bm{\Gamma}_{w}\mathbf{a}=\sup_{\mathbf{a}\in\mathbb{R}^{p}}\int_{-\pi}^{\pi}\mathbf{a}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}_{w}(\omega)\mathbf{a}\,d\omega\leq 2\pi B_{\xi}B_{\chi}.

∎

Proof of Proposition
B.2

(i)
.

In what follows,
C_{k},\,k=1,2,\ldots
denote
positive constants not dependent on the indices
1\leq i,i^{\prime}\leq p
.
By Assumption
B.2

(i)
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}_{\chi}-\mathbf{A}_{\chi}\right\|_{F}^{2}=\frac{1}{p}\sum_{h=1}^{N}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(h)}_{\chi}-\mathbf{A}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right\|_{F}^{2},\quad\text{where}

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(h)}_{\chi}-\mathbf{A}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right\|_{F}=\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{B}}^{(h)}_{\chi}(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{-1}-\mathbf{B}^{(h)}_{\chi}(\mathbf{C}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\widehat{\mathbf{B}}^{(h)}_{\chi}-\mathbf{B}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right)(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{-1}\right\|_{F}+\left\|\mathbf{B}^{(h)}_{\chi}\left((\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{-1}-(\mathbf{C}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{-1}\right)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{B}}^{(h)}_{\chi}-\mathbf{B}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right\|_{F}\;\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{-1}\right\|_{F}+\left\|\mathbf{B}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right\|_{F}\;\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{-1}-(\mathbf{C}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{-1}\right\|_{F}.

(F.25)

By (
F.3
),
Assumption
B.2

(ii)

and that the entries of
(\mathbf{C}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{-1}

are rational functions of those of
\mathbf{C}^{(h)}_{\chi}
,
we have

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq h\leq N}\max\left(\|\mathbf{B}^{(h)}_{\chi}\|_{F},\|\mathbf{C}^{(h)}_{\chi}\|_{F}\right)\leq C_{1}\quad\text{and}\quad\max_{1\leq h\leq N}\|(\mathbf{C}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{-1}\|_{F}\leq C_{2}

(F.26)

Also, by Proposition
F.16

(ii)
,

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq h\leq N}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{B}}^{(h)}_{\chi}-\mathbf{B}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right\|_{F}^{2}\leq(q+1)^{2}\sum_{\ell=1}^{s}\max_{1\leq i,i^{\prime}\leq p}|\widehat{\gamma}_{\chi,ii^{\prime}}(\ell)-\gamma_{\chi,ii^{\prime}}(\ell)|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\frac{sq^{3}p^{4}}{\inf_{\omega}\mu_{\chi,q}^{4}(\omega)}\left(\vartheta_{n,p}^{2}\vee\frac{1}{m^{2}}\vee\frac{1}{p}\right)\right)

(F.27)

and similarly,

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq h\leq N}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(h)}_{\chi}-\mathbf{C}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}\left(\frac{s^{2}q^{3}p^{4}}{\inf_{\omega}\mu_{\chi,q}^{4}(\omega)}\left(\vartheta_{n,p}^{2}\vee\frac{1}{m^{2}}\vee\frac{1}{p}\right)\right).

(F.28)

Also, (
F.26
) and (
F.28
) imply that

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq h\leq N}\left\|\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right)^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq h\leq N}\left\|\left(\mathbf{C}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}+\max_{1\leq h\leq N}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(h)}_{\chi}-\mathbf{C}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}(1)

by Weyl’s inequality, and

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq h\leq N}\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{-1}-(\mathbf{C}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{1\leq h\leq N}\left\|\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{C}}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\left(\mathbf{C}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{F}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(h)}_{\chi}-\mathbf{C}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\frac{p^{2}}{\inf_{\omega}\mu_{\chi,q}^{2}(\omega)}\left(\vartheta_{n,p}\vee\frac{1}{m}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right)\right).

This, together with (
F.25
), (
F.27
), (
F.28
), and that
p=(q+1)N
, proves the first claim.
It also follows that

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq h\leq N}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(h)}_{\chi}-\mathbf{A}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{1\leq h\leq N}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{B}}^{(h)}_{\chi}-\mathbf{B}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right\|_{F}\;\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{-1}\right\|_{F}+\max_{1\leq h\leq N}\left\|\mathbf{B}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right\|_{F}\;\left\|(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{-1}-(\mathbf{C}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{-1}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,O_{P}\left(\vartheta_{n,p}\vee\frac{1}{m}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right).

(F.29)

Then, the second claim follows since

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq i\leq p}\left|\bm{\varphi}_{i}^{\top}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}_{\chi}-\mathbf{A}_{\chi}\right)\right|_{2}\leq\max_{1\leq h\leq N}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(h)}_{\chi}-\mathbf{A}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right\|_{F}.

∎

Let
\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{\mathbb{X}}=(n-s)^{-1}\mathbb{X}\mathbb{X}^{\top}

and
\bm{\Gamma}_{\mathbb{X}}=\mathsf{E}(\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{\mathbb{X}})
, where

\displaystyle\mathbb{X}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{X}_{s+1}&\mathbf{X}_{s+2}&\ldots&\mathbf{X}_{n}\\
&&\ddots&\\
\mathbf{X}_{1}&\mathbf{X}_{2}&\ldots&\mathbf{X}_{n-s}\end{bmatrix}\in\mathbb{R}^{p(s+1)\times(n-s)}.

Then,
\bm{\Gamma}_{\mathbb{X}}=[\bm{\Gamma}_{x}(\ell-\ell^{\prime}),\,1\leq\ell,\ell^{\prime}\leq s+1]
.
Further, we write that
\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{\mathbb{X}}=[\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{x}(\ell,\ell^{\prime}),\,1\leq\ell,\ell^{\prime}\leq s+1]

where
\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{x}(\ell,\ell^{\prime})=[\widehat{\gamma}_{x,ii^{\prime}}(\ell,\ell^{\prime}),\,1\leq i,i^{\prime}\leq p]=(n-s)^{-1}\sum_{t=s+2-\ell}^{n-\ell+1}\mathbf{X}_{t}\mathbf{X}_{t-\ell^{\prime}+\ell}^{\top}
.

Proposition F.22
.

Under the assumptions made in Proposition
B.2

(ii)
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{z}-\bm{\Gamma}_{z}\right\|=O_{P}\left(\vartheta_{n,p}\vee\frac{1}{m}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right),\quad\left|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{z}-\bm{\Gamma}_{z}\right|_{\infty}=O_{P}\left(\vartheta_{n,p}\vee\frac{1}{m}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right).

Proof.

Recall that by Assumption
B.2

(i)
,
s
is finite.
By definition,
\bm{\Gamma}_{z}=\mathbb{A}_{\chi}\bm{\Gamma}_{\mathbb{X}}\mathbb{A}_{\chi}^{\top}

where
\mathbb{A}_{\chi}=[\mathbf{I},-\mathbf{A}_{\chi}]\in\mathbb{R}^{p\times p(s+1)}

and
\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{z}=\widehat{\mathbb{A}}_{\chi}\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{\mathbb{X}}\widehat{\mathbb{A}}_{\chi}^{\top}

with
\widehat{\mathbb{A}}_{\chi}=[\mathbf{I},-\widehat{\mathbf{A}}_{\chi}]
.
Then,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{z}-\bm{\Gamma}_{z}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p}\left\|(\widehat{\mathbb{A}}_{\chi}-\mathbb{A}_{\chi})\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{\mathbb{X}}\widehat{\mathbb{A}}_{\chi}^{\top}\right\|+\frac{1}{p}\left\|\mathbb{A}_{\chi}(\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{\mathbb{X}}-\bm{\Gamma}_{\mathbb{X}})\widehat{\mathbb{A}}_{\chi}^{\top}\right\|+\frac{1}{p}\left\|\mathbb{A}_{\chi}\bm{\Gamma}_{\mathbb{X}}(\widehat{\mathbb{A}}_{\chi}-\mathbb{A}_{\chi})^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle=I+II+III.

From the block structure of
\mathbf{A}_{\chi}
and
the proof of Proposition
B.2

(i)
,

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\mathbb{A}}_{\chi}-\mathbb{A}_{\chi}\right\|=\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}_{\chi}-\mathbf{A}_{\chi}\right\|\leq s\cdot\max_{1\leq h\leq N}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(h)}_{\chi}-\mathbf{A}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right\|_{F}=O_{P}\left(\vartheta_{n,p}\vee\frac{1}{m}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right).

(F.30)

With
C_{2}
and
C_{3}
given in (
F.26
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{A}_{\chi}\right\|\leq 1+s\cdot\max_{1\leq h\leq N}\left\|\mathbf{B}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right\|_{F}\;\left\|(\mathbf{C}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{-1}\right\|_{F}\leq 1+sC_{2}C_{3}<\infty.

(F.31)

By Proposition 2.3 of
Basu and Michailidis, 2015
, Assumption
2.1
and Proposition
2.1
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p}\left\|\bm{\Gamma}_{\mathbb{X}}\right\|\leq 2\pi\sup_{\omega}\frac{\mu_{x,1}(\omega)}{p}\leq 2\pi\sup_{\omega}\left(\beta_{\chi,1}(\omega)+\frac{B_{\xi}}{p}\right)<\infty.

(F.32)

Also by the arguments analogous to those adopted in the proof of Lemma
F.5

(i)
,
there exists a fixed constant
C>0
such that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p^{2}}\mathsf{E}\left(\left\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{\mathbb{X}}-\bm{\Gamma}_{\mathbb{X}}\right\|_{F}^{2}\right)=\frac{1}{p^{2}}\sum_{\ell,\ell^{\prime}=1}^{s+1}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}=1}^{p}\mathsf{E}\left(\left|\widehat{\gamma}_{x,ii^{\prime}}(\ell,\ell^{\prime})-\gamma_{x,ii^{\prime}}(\ell-\ell^{\prime}))\right|^{2}\right)\leq C\frac{(s+1)^{2}}{n}.

(F.33)

Then by (
F.30
)–(
F.33
),

I=O_{P}(\vartheta_{n,p}\vee m^{-1}\vee p^{-1/2})
,
by (
F.30
)–(
F.31
) and (
F.33
),

II=O_{P}(n^{-1/2})
and
III
is bounded analogously as
I
, such that

p^{-1}\|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{z}-\bm{\Gamma}_{z}\|=O_{P}(\vartheta_{n,p}\vee m^{-1}\vee p^{-1/2})
.

For the second claim, we proceed similarly by noting that

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{z}-\bm{\Gamma}_{z}\right|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i,i^{\prime}}\left\|\bm{\varphi}_{i}^{\top}\left(\widehat{\mathbb{A}}_{\chi}-\mathbb{A}_{\chi}\right)\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{\mathbb{X}}\widehat{\mathbb{A}}_{\chi}^{\top}\bm{\varphi}_{i^{\prime}}\right\|+\max_{i,i^{\prime}}\left\|\bm{\varphi}_{i}^{\top}\mathbb{A}_{\chi}\left(\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{\mathbb{X}}-\bm{\Gamma}_{\mathbb{X}}\right)\widehat{\mathbb{A}}_{\chi}^{\top}\bm{\varphi}_{i^{\prime}}\right\|

\displaystyle+\max_{i,i^{\prime}}\left\|\bm{\varphi}_{i}^{\top}\mathbb{A}_{\chi}\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{\mathbb{X}}\left(\widehat{\mathbb{A}}_{\chi}-\mathbb{A}_{\chi}\right)^{\top}\bm{\varphi}_{i^{\prime}}\right\|=IV+V+VI.

From the block structure of
\mathbf{A}_{\chi}
,
the second claim of Proposition
B.2

(i)
,
Lemmas
F.2
and
F.5

(ii)

and (
F.30
)–(
F.31
),

IV=O_{P}(\vartheta_{n,p}\vee m^{-1}\vee p^{-1/2})
,
and
V
and
VI
are bounded analogously.
∎

Proof of Proposition
B.2

(ii)
.

Denote the eigendecomposition of
\bm{\Gamma}_{v}
by

\bm{\Gamma}_{v}=\mathbf{E}_{v}\bm{\mathcal{M}}_{v}\mathbf{E}_{v}^{\top}
.
With
\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{z}
as
\widehat{\mathbf{S}}
and
\bm{\Gamma}_{v}
as
\mathbf{S}
,
(
F.6
)–(
F.9
) are met by
Assumption
B.3
, Proposition
B.1

and Proposition
F.22

with
\varrho_{1}=\ldots=\varrho_{q}=1
and

\omega_{n,p}^{(1)}=\omega_{n,p}^{(2)}=(\vartheta_{n,p}\vee m^{-1}\vee p^{-1/2})
.
Therefore, applying Lemmas
F.6
and
F.8
,
there exists a diagonal, orthogonal matrix
\bm{\mathcal{O}}\in\mathbb{R}^{p\times p}
such that

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{z,1:q}-\mathbf{E}_{v}\bm{\mathcal{O}}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\vartheta_{n,p}\vee\frac{1}{m}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right),

\displaystyle\sqrt{p}\max_{1\leq i\leq p}\left|\bm{\varphi}_{i}^{\top}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{z,1:q}-\mathbf{E}_{v}\bm{\mathcal{O}}\right)\right|_{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\vartheta_{n,p}\vee\frac{1}{m}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right).

Also, by Assumptions
B.1
and
B.2

(i)
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i}\,\mathsf{Var}(V_{it})=\max_{i}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\mu_{v,j}|e_{v,ij}|^{2}=\max_{i}\sum_{j=1}^{q}R_{ij}^{2}\mathsf{Var}(u_{ij})\leq q(B_{\chi}^{(1)})^{2}<\infty

(F.34)

where
\mathbf{e}_{v,j}=(e_{v,ij},\,1\leq i\leq p)^{\top}
and
\mathbf{R}=[R_{ij},\,1\leq i\leq p,\,1\leq j\leq q]
,
such that
\max_{1\leq i\leq p}|e_{v,ij}|=O(p^{-1/2})

by Assumption
B.3
.
Besides, by (
F.29
),
Lemma
F.2
and Chebyshev’s inequality,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}-\mathbf{Z}_{t}\right\|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\left\|\sum_{\ell=1}^{s}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}_{\chi,\ell}-\mathbf{A}_{\chi,\ell})\mathbf{X}_{t-\ell}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\sum_{\ell=1}^{s}\left\|[(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}^{(h)}_{\chi,\ell}-\mathbf{A}^{(h)}_{\chi,\ell})\mathbf{X}^{(h)}_{t-\ell},\,1\leq h\leq N]\right\|=O_{P}\left(\vartheta_{n,p}\vee\frac{1}{m}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right)

for given
t
, where
\mathbf{X}^{(h)}_{t}
is defined analogously as
\bm{\chi}^{(h)}_{t}
.
Also, from the finiteness of
\|\mathbb{A}_{\chi}\|
in (
F.31
)
and (
F.23
), we have
\|\mathbf{Z}_{t}\|=O_{P}(\sqrt{p})

for a given
t
.
Finally,
\|\mathsf{Cov}(\mathbf{E}_{v}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{t})\|\leq B_{w}
by Proposition
B.1

such that
\|\mathbf{E}_{v}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{t}\|=O_{P}(1)
by Chebyshev’s inequality.
Putting the above observations together,

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{\mathbf{R}}\widehat{\mathbf{u}}_{t}-\mathbf{R}\mathbf{u}_{t}\right|_{\infty}\leq

\displaystyle\max_{i}\left|\bm{\varphi}_{i}^{\top}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{z,1:q}\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{z,1:q}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}-\mathbf{E}_{v}\mathbf{E}_{v}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}_{t}\right)\right|+\max_{i}\left|\bm{\varphi}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{v}\mathbf{E}_{v}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{t}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{i}\left|\bm{\varphi}_{i}^{\top}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{z,1:q}-\mathbf{E}_{v}\bm{\mathcal{O}}\right)\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{z,1:q}^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}\right|+\max_{i}\left|\bm{\varphi}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{v}\bm{\mathcal{O}}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{z,1:q}-\mathbf{E}_{v}\bm{\mathcal{O}}\right)^{\top}\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}\right|

\displaystyle+\max_{i}\left|\bm{\varphi}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{E}_{v}\mathbf{E}_{v}^{\top}(\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}-\mathbf{Z}_{t})\right|+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right)=O_{P}\left(\vartheta_{n,p}\vee\frac{1}{m}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right).

∎

Proof of Proposition
B.2

(iii)
.

We can write each block in (
B.1
) as

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\chi}^{(h)}_{t}\\
\bm{\chi}^{(h)}_{t-1}\\
\vdots\\
\bm{\chi}^{(h)}_{t-s+1}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{A}^{(h)}_{\chi,1}&\mathbf{A}^{(h)}_{\chi,2}&\ldots&\mathbf{A}^{(h)}_{\chi,s-1}&\mathbf{A}^{(h)}_{\chi,s}\\
\mathbf{I}&\mathbf{O}&&\mathbf{O}&\mathbf{O}\\
&&\ddots&&\\
\mathbf{O}&\mathbf{O}&\ldots&\mathbf{I}&\mathbf{O}\end{bmatrix}}_{\mathfrak{A}^{(h)}_{\chi}}\;\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\chi}^{(h)}_{t-1}\\
\bm{\chi}^{(h)}_{t-2}\\
\vdots\\
\bm{\chi}^{(h)}_{t-s}\end{bmatrix}+\underbrace{\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{R}^{(h)}\mathbf{u}_{t}\\
\mathbf{0}\\
\vdots\\
\mathbf{0}\end{bmatrix}}_{\mathfrak{R}^{(h)}\mathbf{u}_{t}},\text{ such that}

\displaystyle\bm{\chi}^{(h)}_{t}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{I}-\mathfrak{A}^{(h)}_{\chi}L)^{-1}\mathfrak{R}^{(h)}\mathbf{u}_{t}=\mathbf{J}\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}(\mathfrak{A}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{\ell}\mathfrak{R}^{(h)}\mathbf{u}_{t-\ell}

with
\mathbf{J}=[\mathbf{I}_{q+1},\mathbf{O},\ldots,\mathbf{O}]\in\mathbb{R}^{(q+1)\times(q+1)s}
.
Then, defining
\mathbf{B}^{(h)}_{\ell}
that consists of
the
(q+1)
rows of
\mathbf{B}_{\ell}
to satisfy

\bm{\chi}^{(h)}_{t}=\sum_{\ell=0}^{\infty}\mathbf{B}^{(h)}_{\ell}\mathbf{u}_{t-\ell}

under (
1
),
we have
\mathbf{B}^{(h)}_{\ell}=\mathbf{J}(\mathfrak{A}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{\ell}\mathfrak{R}^{(h)}
.
Letting
\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}^{(h)}_{\chi}
and
\widehat{\mathfrak{R}}^{(h)}_{\chi}

denote the estimated counterparts of

\mathfrak{A}^{(h)}_{\chi}
and
\mathfrak{R}^{(h)}_{\chi}
, respectively,
we have

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{\bm{\chi}}^{\text{{unr}}}_{t+a|t}-\bm{\chi}_{t+a|t}\right|_{\infty}\leq

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq h\leq N}\left|\mathbf{J}\sum_{\ell=0}^{K}\left[(\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{\ell}\widehat{\mathfrak{R}}^{(h)}_{\chi}\widehat{\mathbf{u}}_{t-\ell}-(\mathfrak{A}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{\ell}\mathfrak{R}^{(h)}_{\chi}\mathbf{u}_{t-\ell}\right]\right|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\left|\sum_{\ell=K+1}^{\infty}\mathbf{B}_{\ell+a}\mathbf{u}_{t-\ell}\right|_{\infty}=I+II.

From (
F.29
) and (
F.31
),
we obtain

\displaystyle\max_{h}\left\|(\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{\ell}-(\mathfrak{A}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{\ell}\right\|\leq\max_{h}\left\|\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right\|\;\left\|(\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{\ell-1}-(\mathfrak{A}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{\ell-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\max_{h}\left\|({\mathfrak{A}}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{\ell-1}\right\|\;\left\|\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}^{(h)}_{\chi}-\mathfrak{A}^{(h)}_{\chi}\right\|=O_{P}\left(\max_{h}\|\mathfrak{A}^{(h)}_{\chi}\|^{\ell-1}\left(\vartheta_{n,p}\vee\frac{1}{m}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right)\right)

by induction for any finite
\ell
, while

\displaystyle\max_{h}\left|\widehat{\mathfrak{R}}^{(h)}_{\chi}\widehat{\mathbf{u}}_{t-\ell}-\mathfrak{R}^{(h)}_{\chi}\mathbf{u}_{t-\ell}\right|_{\infty}=O_{P}\left(\vartheta_{n,p}\vee\frac{1}{m}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right)

by Proposition
B.2

(ii)
.
From these observations and (
F.34
) (in combination with Chebyshev’s inequality),
we derive

\displaystyle I\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{K}\left\{\max_{h}\left|\mathbf{J}\left[(\widehat{\mathfrak{A}}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{\ell}-(\mathfrak{A}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{\ell}\right]\;\widehat{\mathfrak{R}}^{(h)}_{\chi}\widehat{\mathbf{u}}_{t-\ell}\right|_{\infty}+\max_{h}\left|\mathbf{J}(\mathfrak{A}^{(h)}_{\chi})^{\ell}\left(\widehat{\mathfrak{R}}^{(h)}_{\chi}\widehat{\mathbf{u}}_{t-\ell}-\mathfrak{R}^{(h)}_{\chi}\mathbf{u}_{t-\ell}\right)\right|_{\infty}\right\}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(K\max_{h}\|\mathfrak{A}^{(h)}_{\chi}\|^{K}\left(\vartheta_{n,p}\vee\frac{1}{m}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}\right)\right).

Also, from Assumption
2.2
, there exist constants
C_{\nu},C_{\nu,\Xi,\varsigma}>0

that depend only on their subscripts such that

\displaystyle\left\|\left|\sum_{\ell=K+1}^{\infty}\mathbf{B}_{\ell+a}\mathbf{u}_{t-\ell}\right|_{\infty}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}\leq C_{\nu}\log(p)\sum_{\ell=K+1}^{\infty}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\left\|\left|\mathbf{B}_{\ell+a,\cdot j}u_{j,t-\ell}\right|_{\infty}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\nu}\log(p)\sum_{\ell=K+1}^{\infty}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\left|\mathbf{B}_{\ell+a,\cdot j}\right|_{\infty}^{2}\left\|\left|\mathbf{u}_{t-\ell}\right|_{\infty}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}\leq C_{\nu}\log(p)q^{2/\nu}\mu_{\nu}^{2/\nu}\sum_{\ell=K+1}^{\infty}\Xi^{2}(1+\ell+a)^{-2\varsigma}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\nu,\Xi,\varsigma}\log(p)q^{2/\nu}\mu_{\nu}^{2/\nu}(K+a)^{-2(\varsigma-1)},

by Lemma D.3 of
Zhang and Wu, 2021
.
Therefore by Chebyshev’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle II=O_{P}\left(\log^{1/2}(p)q^{1/\nu}\mu_{\nu}^{1/\nu}(K+a)^{-\varsigma+1}\right)

under Assumption
F.1

(iii)

(iii)(a)
.
When Assumption
F.1

(iii)

(iii)(b)
is met,

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq i\leq p}\mathsf{Var}\left(\sum_{\ell=K+1}^{\infty}\mathbf{B}_{\ell+a,i\cdot}\mathbf{u}_{t-\ell}\right)=\max_{i}\sum_{\ell=K+1}^{\infty}\left|\mathbf{B}_{\ell+a,i\cdot}\right|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\ell=K+1}^{\infty}\Xi^{2}(1+\ell+a)^{-2\varsigma}\leq C_{\Xi,\varsigma}(K+a)^{-2(\varsigma-1)}

such that
II=O_{P}(\log^{1/2}(p)K^{-\varsigma+1})
.
Combining the bounds on
I
and
II
, the conclusion follows.
∎
